Week recap and lookahead\",\"created_at\":\"2026-07-30T18:06:52.929Z\",\"published_at\":\"2026-07-31T16:05:33.102Z\",\"updated_at\":\"2026-08-10T09:28:53.361Z\",\"id\":203756392234241,\"uuid\":\"d2699898-e24a-479e-aeb5-e87e4d595d0f\",\"content\":{\"_uid\":\"780a6b79-aa79-4bc1-ac7a-cd33cdff94d9\",\"body\":[{\"_uid\":\"a37a11c5-259b-48ac-939a-1f1dedde8cf8\",\"body\":{\"type\":\"doc\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Thank you for spending AI Week with us. We’re thrilled by the reaction and we all enjoyed replying to your questions. Thanks for engaging.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Some of my favorite quotes from \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"LinkedIn and \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://www.linkedin.com/posts/evgeni-john-biriukov-04276a82_grafana-observability-aiagents-share-7487734574739935232-6_vv/?utm_source=share\u0026utm_medium=member_desktop\u0026rcm=ACoAAA6Y2WwBc350hfJDWnvlYkSoJv2pg2Qa5-w\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\"Reddit\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://www.reddit.com/r/grafana/comments/1v928us/comment/p0c9wnl/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" include:\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"“Grafana’s latest announcements aren’t just 'AI features.' They signal a shift in what observability platforms are becoming.”\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}},{\"type\":\"italic\"}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"“I had Claude migrate and update and validate dozens of hand-rolled dashboards… Worked astonishingly well… 95% done in 30m with the MCP and Claude.”\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}},{\"type\":\"italic\"}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":2,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Good vibes\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}},{\"type\":\"bold\"}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"W\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"hile AI Week is coming to a close, the story doesn't end here. Just like you, we're in the middle of a major transition in how AI and observability work together to solve problems quickly.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1F1F1F\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"We can’t do it without you. So please try our tools in your own teams, and let us know what we got right, as well as where we need to keep iterating.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\" \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1F1F1F\"}}]},{\"text\":\"We know lots of people already feel left behind by the rate of change, so we’re making it our mission to catch you up, filter out all the noise, and bring you the very best in agentic operations.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"That's why I'd like to use this last post for the week to look back at how we got here and look ahead at what's yet to come—for Grafana Labs' AI tooling, for the software development and management industry, and most importantly, for users like you.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1F1F1F\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":2,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Our story\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}},{\"type\":\"bold\"}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"At Grafana Labs, we've always tried to innovate at the cutting edge of observability to solve real problems brought to us by our internal and external users. But when it comes to LLMs, we were a little late to the party. To be completely honest, \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"we were skeptical\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/blog/ai-in-observability-at-grafana-labs-making-observability-easy-and-accessible-for-everyone/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":\"_self\",\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\". Starting in 2024, we began seeing so many examples of AI features being crammed in where they didn’t belong. And even when the use case made sense, the tools seemed incapable of executing the actual task. \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"We realized things had changed when we started seeing LLMs running by themselves in loops; when agents were born.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"There is little doubt that agents have changed the way we build software. You don’t have to look far to find examples of people who have been supercharged by Claude, Codex, or Copilot, even if they aren’t \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"loop-maxing\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}},{\"type\":\"italic\"}]},{\"text\":\", \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"mob-vibing\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}},{\"type\":\"italic\"}]},{\"text\":\", or pitting agent against agent in \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"adversarial arenas\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}},{\"type\":\"italic\"}]},{\"text\":\". \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"We are building more and shipping more thanks to agents. Not only that, we’re also working in entirely new ways. What do you do while your agents are \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"thinking\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}},{\"type\":\"italic\"}]},{\"text\":\"? Some engineers are working on multiple projects at the same time. And I mean that literally; while one agent cooks, they are prompting another. \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"The rate of change has dramatically increased, and change, as we all know, is where trouble starts. Each engineer is now producing more operational demand than ever before and this shows no signs of slowing down. Naturally, at Grafana Labs, we feel like it falls to us to help you and your teams adapt and thrive in this new world.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"The AI team at Grafana Labs has grown significantly over the past year, but we remain dedicated to the experimentation and innovation that is already helping thousands of users today. We will continue to hold ourselves to the highest standard: we will never ship AI slop tools, or AI for the sake of it. To do this properly, we need your help. Your feedback, good and bad, helps ensure we’re solving real problems and it helps us continue to test our assumptions.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":2,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"A recap of AI Week\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}},{\"type\":\"bold\"}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Here’s a refresher of how the week that’s a wrap for our first AI Week. Let us know how you think it went, what you’d like more of, and where we can improve if we do this again.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"On a personal note, I hope you see the dedication and creative spirit that the team is pouring into our new tools. We’re having a lot of fun building in this exciting space, and we can’t wait to hear from you after you have had time to kick the tires.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":2,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"What’s next?\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}},{\"type\":\"bold\"}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"So, where do we go from here? Things are continuing to change out there at an exciting and daunting pace. We’re starting to trust AI on more meaningful tasks, and we’re seeing places it can run reliably as part of our workflows. We’re already impressed by some of the skills and MCP combinations people have set up for Assistant and Assistant Investigations, integrating it even deeper into their systems and teams.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"At Grafana Labs, we’re shaping a future where AI removes the daily operational grind, empowering engineers to focus on what’s next. As telemetry insights become universally accessible across the organization, we anticipate a shift toward more data-driven requests and deeper, enlightened discussions, ultimately allowing us to make better decisions.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Auto-improvement loops can help optimize code based on your telemetry (which could be anything from infra metrics to business data), run A/B tests, and roll out the winning combination. Bug reports from users can be pre-investigated before a human has even seen it, enriching the support experience and speeding up resolution. Automated agents can carry out janitorial duties and generate intelligent reports that land in the right place at the right time.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"It's a lot to adapt to, but we plan to be there with you every step of the way. change. \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":2,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Reducing \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}}]},{\"text\":\"Grafana Labs’ 2026 Observability Survey\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/observability-survey/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\", operational complexity and overhead are the top observability challenges for organizations around the world. While AI is creating new opportunities for automation and insight, it’s also reshaping the systems teams need to operate. Telemetry volumes keep growing, systems are increasingly distributed, and organizations are managing more data, more dependencies, and new points of failure that can be difficult to detect and troubleshoot. \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Helping organizations address these challenges, as well as get more value from AI, has been a major focus for us over the past year. Here’s a quick look at how that focus has shaped Grafana Cloud. \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":3,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Grafana Assistant: actually useful AI for your observability strategy \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}},{\"type\":\"bold\"}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Grafana Assistant\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/products/cloud/ai-assistant/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\", the context-aware AI agent within Grafana Cloud, helps teams investigate incidents, write queries, and understand observability data using natural language. Assistant, which \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}}]},{\"text\":\"became generally available last year\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/blog/grafana-assistant-ga-assistant-investigations-preview/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\", answers questions with the telemetry you already have and uses \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}}]},{\"text\":\"the knowledge graph\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/docs/grafana-cloud/knowledge-graph/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" to connect signals to services, dependencies, and changes, helping accelerate root cause analysis.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Over the past several months, \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}}]},{\"text\":\"we’ve expanded Assistant\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/blog/grafana-assistant-everywhere/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" to meet teams wherever they work, including self-managed Grafana environments, collaboration tools like Slack and Microsoft Teams, and through APIs and CLIs. We also introduced Assistant skills and automations, enabling teams to customize AI workflows, automate routine tasks, and gain insights from their observability data more efficiently.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"And as AI becomes a bigger part of incident response, we \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}}]},{\"text\":\"continue to evolve Assistant Investigations\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/blog/automatically-discover-and-remediate-root-causes-with-grafana-assistant-investigations/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\", an autonomous investigation feature that can automatically identify potential root causes and recommend steps for remediation.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":3,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"AI Observability to monitor agentic workflows\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}},{\"type\":\"bold\"}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Just as much as teams need to use AI for faster troubleshooting and analysis, they also need a way to observe the LLMs and agents they’re running in production environments. To that end, we \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1A1A2E\"}}]},{\"text\":\"introduced AI Observability\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/blog/ai-observability-for-agents-in-grafana-cloud/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\", The research enables organizations to get the most from market analysis in alignment with their unique business and technology needs.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"What does it mean to be named a Leader?\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}},{\"type\":\"bold\"}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Leaders execute well against their current vision and are well positioned for tomorrow. to build a trust platform for your agent with Grafana Agent Observability\",\"created_at\":\"2026-07-29T19:54:02.022Z\",\"published_at\":\"2026-07-30T15:06:46.813Z\",\"updated_at\":\"2026-08-08T06:58:05.871Z\",\"id\":203428831402215,\"uuid\":\"fc609d7e-9bc1-429b-937d-3cf186154057\",\"content\":{\"_uid\":\"dbeb9e4d-3e7a-4fd7-b97a-9279d11e78cc\",\"body\":[{\"_uid\":\"2254c07a-08cf-4d32-994c-0007f0e52877\",\"body\":{\"type\":\"doc\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Observing fast-growing agentic workloads is no small feat, especially if you try to build your own monitoring stack or rely solely on tools built for a time before LLMs.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"At Grafana Labs, we know this all too well. \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Grafana Assistant went from an internal \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"hackathon project\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/blog/llm-grafana-assistant/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" to \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"generally available\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/blog/grafana-assistant-ga-assistant-investigations-preview/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" in a little over six months. And while it was great to see how eager our users were to adopt it, that interest came with plenty of growing pains as we managed scaling and complexity behind the scenes. Reviewing contributions to the prompt, tool set, and agent harness became a nightmare as changes piled on from multiple teams. Bespoke dashboards just weren’t cutting it. (You can read more about our experience in \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"this post on Unprompted\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://medium.com/grafana-labs/building-an-evaluation-loop-for-grafana-assistant-9a8690d8662d\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\", our new engineering blog.)\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"If you've been building agents, you've probably faced some similar frustrations. The good news? You don't have to blindly walk through the same growing pains that we did. We built \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"Agent Observability\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/products/cloud/ai-observability/?pg=explore-what-s-next-in-agentic-operations-introducing-ai-week\u0026plcmt=in-text#agent-observability\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" as an internal tool to address these challenges, and today we're making it generally available to Grafana Cloud users to help you watch your agents, test their behavior, and step in when something's off. \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"But having the right tool is only the first step. This post will document guidance and best practices for building a robust trust and monitoring setup to monitor your own agents, based on the phases we went through building Assistant.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":2,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Phase 0: Early prototype and initial deployment\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"It's exciting to start with a fresh idea, pick an agent framework, and say the first “Hello, world!” to a new agent. In fact, you can get surprisingly far by adding tools and single-shot examples to the system prompt, then doing some simple Q\u0026A testing.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"After getting the agent to a place where it generally feels solid and helpful, you can run an initial benchmark or test suite with a barebones set of use cases the agent needs to address. But once it’s deployed to production for the first time and the first issue appears, the cracks start to show. A customer may reach out regarding a strange conversation or an internal user might message on Slack with a screenshot of a conversation that went off the rails. How do you begin to troubleshoot this?\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"The next step in development is making sure that these conversations can be found and triaged efficiently. Like traditional software monitoring, this is done through instrumentation of the agent’s code.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":2,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Phase 1: Monitoring live traffic\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"There are two main facets that are important to monitor when observing an agent. The first is traditional engineering metrics such as latency, cost, number of tokens, and errors. These metrics can give developers a sense of the general maintainability of the agent, identify cost anomalies, and do an initial round of improvements to the agent, such as reformatting the prompt to introduce prompt caching and save on tokens. When the finance department comes down to the engineering team and asks for the costs to maintain an agent, this step will provide the answer.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]}]},\"component\":\"legacy_richtext\"},{\"_uid\":\"d15b2d6f-0980-4e96-9ba4-b12cc1ff2d63\",\"media\":{\"id\":203431002413764,\"alt\":\"Token and cost usage dashboard in Grafana Agent Observability\",\"name\":\"\",\"focus\":\"\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"filename\":\"https://a-us.storyblok.com/f/1022730/1999x585/f61e148966/agent-observability-best-practices-token-cost.png\",\"copyright\":\"\",\"fieldtype\":\"asset\",\"meta_data\":{\"alt\":\"Token and cost usage dashboard in Grafana Agent Observability\",\"size\":\"1999x585\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"copyright\":\"\"},\"is_external_url\":false},\"caption\":\"Token and cost usage dashboard in Grafana Agent Observability\",\"alignment\":\"\",\"component\":\"legacy_image\"},{\"_uid\":\"63f8e25b-b627-453c-ae78-336a15d7cdfc\",\"body\":{\"type\":\"doc\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"The second facet is the conversations themselves. You can surface a surprising amount of feedback by spot-checking conversations and seeing how the agent behaves without any deeper analysis. Reading over a handful of transcripts gives a great general sense of how the agent is working. Instrumenting the conversations also makes it easy to triage customer requests, find errant conversations, and resolve them.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]}]},\"component\":\"legacy_richtext\"},{\"_uid\":\"8e38487f-ff77-4de6-81d4-b24abd519b6b\",\"media\":{\"id\":203431002319549,\"alt\":\"Conversation list view in Grafana Agent list view in Grafana Agent Observability\",\"size\":\"1999x1012\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"copyright\":\"\"},\"is_external_url\":false},\"caption\":\"Conversation list view in Grafana Agent set up instrumentation and visibility into an agent’s conversations, it is as simple as writing a config that fits in with agent frameworks, through the \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"Grafana Agent Observability SDK.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://github.com/grafana/agento11y\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" The SDK ships with \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"coding agent skills\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://github.com/grafana/agento11y#quick-start-with-an-ai-coding-agent\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" to help with instrumentation. \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"gcx\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/blog/telemetry-driven-development-how-to-gain-confidence-in-your-coding-agents-behavior-with-gcx-and-grafana-mcp/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\", Grafana's new CLI, also comes with skills to help get these phases set up (see \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"agento11y-instrument\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://github.com/grafana/gcx/blob/main/claude-plugin/skills/agento11y-instrument/SKILL.md\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" for more details).\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Great, now there is a way to respond to issues that are raised with the agent in production. However, spot checking and traditional engineering metrics can only get a team so far. The next step is surfacing anomalies automatically, for which there are some stronger methods available.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":2,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Phase 2: Evaluating baseline performance\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Agents are inherently non-deterministic and heavily customized for a wide range of use cases. This combination makes it hard to get a sense of agent quality using traditional metrics. How do you trust that they are working correctly in production? \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"To address this, a combination of deterministic checks (such as regex or JSON validation) and LLM-judge evaluators is recommended for monitoring agent quality over time. LLM-judge evaluators work by asking another LLM to take a look at the transcript, or pieces of the transcript such as an individual tool call, and score it on an output scale, typically boolean (pass/fail) or Likert (1-5).\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Evaluator\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/docs/grafana-cloud/observe-and-act/agent-observability/guides/evaluation/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" templates available out of the box in Grafana, such as \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"PII identification\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/docs/grafana-cloud/machine-learning/agent-observability/privacy-and-security/pii-and-secrets-redaction/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" and \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"Toxicity\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"code\"}]},{\"text\":\", are useful to get started. While these general LLM-judge evaluators like \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"Helpfulness\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"code\"}]},{\"text\":\" can give you more information about an agent's performance than pure engineering metrics, they \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"can be prone to unreliability\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://arxiv.org/html/2606.19544v1#bib.bib1\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\". \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"That’s where custom evaluators can help tailor the metrics to more accurately diagnose agent behavior. When creating an evaluator, configure \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"the prompt and the context from the conversation\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/docs/grafana-cloud/machine-learning/agent-observability/guides/evaluation/#write-an-llm-judge-prompt\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" that's passed to the judge model to help improve the accuracy of a judge for a specific agent’s use case. Evaluator rules give full flexibility regarding when to run these evaluations over live traffic (for instance, running over the full conversation transcript or just individual agent messages within a conversation). Prompt injection detection, custom fulfillment scores, and even identifying conversations covering a certain use case or topic are all configurable within Grafana Cloud.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"To supplement LLM-judges, deterministic evaluators like regexes can flag conversations containing a specific word. For example, you could check for conversations recommending a competitor product or leaking an API key. \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]}]},\"component\":\"legacy_richtext\"},{\"_uid\":\"e0ffd174-0cc1-4698-8f35-4704df3e8bcb\",\"media\":{\"id\":203431002368705,\"alt\":\"Conversation-level evaluators in a conversation\",\"name\":\"\",\"focus\":\"\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"filename\":\"https://a-us.storyblok.com/f/1022730/1806x1002/0356940168/agent-observability-best-practices-conversation-eval.png\",\"copyright\":\"\",\"fieldtype\":\"asset\",\"meta_data\":{\"alt\":\"Conversation-level evaluators in a conversation\",\"size\":\"1806x1002\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"copyright\":\"\"},\"is_external_url\":false},\"caption\":\"Conversation-level evaluators in a conversation\",\"alignment\":\"\",\"component\":\"legacy_image\"},{\"_uid\":\"002e6031-afbc-40bc-9d87-f967a334b339\",\"body\":{\"type\":\"doc\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Within Agent Observability, \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"evaluators\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/docs/grafana-cloud/machine-learning/agent-observability/guides/evaluation\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" also publish metrics to Grafana Cloud Metrics, enabling custom dashboards to go along with the aggregate dashboards already there. Having the metrics in Cloud Metrics also unlocks alerting through a \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"dedicated Grafana Alerting integration\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/docs/grafana-cloud/machine-learning/agent-observability/guides/evaluation/#create-alerts-from-evaluation-rules\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\". That way, if a change is pushed to the agent that tanks performance in front of production customers, the on-call engineer can be alerted to revert it.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]}]},\"component\":\"legacy_richtext\"},{\"_uid\":\"f4b5189b-9543-44bb-a477-6349eff3e4ad\",\"media\":{\"id\":203431002299068,\"alt\":\"Evaluator scores over time for a chat scores over time for a chat application\",\"size\":\"1999x894\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"copyright\":\"\"},\"is_external_url\":false},\"caption\":\"Evaluator scores over time for a chat application\",\"alignment\":\"\",\"component\":\"legacy_image\"},{\"_uid\":\"15540e12-3c78-4201-aa18-2256fe33f0f9\",\"body\":{\"type\":\"doc\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Once evaluators are monitoring live traffic, developers can have more trust in their agent out in the wild and more confidence that issues will be proactively identified.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"The next step is supporting iteration on the agent without introducing regressions.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":2,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Phase 3: Building a more robust test suite\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"As mentioned in Phase 0, you can establish enough confidence for your first deployment through benchmarking and building a test suite of core reference use cases that the agent needs to handle. But this should not be the end of the story.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Test suites are ultimately just collections of test cases. A simple test case consists of an initial conversation prompt and the expected output result. These are used as the seed prompts to test an agent and give a rubric for responses to evaluate for improvement or regressions.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"A conversation that fails or gets a low score from an evaluator is especially valuable. These failures can  highlight edge cases and broader areas of necessary improvement for an agent, showing developers where to focus their efforts. Evaluators can be assigned an \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"action\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/docs/grafana-cloud/observe-and-act/agent-observability/guides/evaluation/#route-conversations-with-actions\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" to identify conversations that fit these patterns and route them into a \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"collection\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/docs/grafana-cloud/observe-and-act/agent-observability/guides/conversations/#save-conversations\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\". Once this collection of conversations that fit the pattern has been assembled, it can be annotated by a human or agent and pushed into a \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"test suite\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/docs/grafana-cloud/observe-and-act/agent-observability/guides/experiments/#build-and-version-test-suites\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\".\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]}]},\"component\":\"legacy_richtext\"},{\"_uid\":\"88320c56-cfa9-42e0-8da5-ae75bec287ae\",\"media\":{\"id\":203431002335935,\"alt\":\"Flow for collecting low-scoring conversations\",\"name\":\"\",\"focus\":\"\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"filename\":\"https://a-us.storyblok.com/f/1022730/1096x1044/6e361a2d0a/agent-observability-best-practices-flow.png\",\"copyright\":\"\",\"fieldtype\":\"asset\",\"meta_data\":{\"alt\":\"Flow for collecting low-scoring conversations\",\"size\":\"1096x1044\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"copyright\":\"\"},\"is_external_url\":false},\"caption\":\"Flow for collecting low-scoring conversations\",\"alignment\":\"\",\"component\":\"legacy_image\"},{\"_uid\":\"aa012f67-9a9a-48d8-846f-aa9192365ebd\",\"body\":{\"type\":\"doc\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Once you have a strong test suite foundation of core use cases, failure cases observed in production, and ideas in mind to improve the agent, you can start kicking off offline evaluations and experiments.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":2,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Phase 4: Tracking experiments and moving to CI/CD\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"\\\"Offline evaluation\\\" is a term for evaluating an agent against a set of test cases and producing a report on its performance. This is different from online evaluation, where we are evaluating live traffic as it comes in. Offline evaluation is helpful for initial agent development, benchmarking, validating changes to an agent, and CI/CD.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]}]},\"component\":\"legacy_richtext\"},{\"_uid\":\"5b471174-b890-4343-a404-cc97a6ee72d2\",\"media\":{\"id\":203431002335934,\"alt\":\"Experiments list view in Grafana Agent Observability\",\"name\":\"\",\"focus\":\"\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"filename\":\"https://a-us.storyblok.com/f/1022730/1999x1102/d5fb6fa7db/agent-observability-best-practices-experiments.png\",\"copyright\":\"\",\"fieldtype\":\"asset\",\"meta_data\":{\"alt\":\"Experiments list view in Grafana Agent Observability\",\"size\":\"1999x1102\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"copyright\":\"\"},\"is_external_url\":false},\"caption\":\"Experiments list view in Grafana Agent Observability\",\"alignment\":\"\",\"component\":\"legacy_image\"},{\"_uid\":\"9b84c1c2-b14a-4b7c-b364-864f8f1980f7\",\"body\":{\"type\":\"doc\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Benchmarking helps determine if an agent or standalone LLM performs well at a certain, well known task. For example, Grafana’s \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"o11y-bench\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://o11ybench.ai/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" measures how well an agent performs over a suite of ~70 tasks related to observability within the Grafana ecosystem. As new frontier models are released, benchmarking can help inform which models are best suited for which tasks relevant to your industry.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Verifying agent changes and CI/CD are more relevant to developers actively iterating on a agent. Having a platform that makes it easy for both developers and product managers to make changes to the agent and see how the agent performs over the test suite is crucial for rapid iteration. The most common use case is comparing an agent’s performance over the test suite before and after a prompt change to determine if the change is safe to deploy to production. \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Run \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"Experiments\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/docs/grafana-cloud/observe-and-act/agent-observability/guides/experiments/#experiments\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" using the \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"agento11y SDK\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://github.com/grafana/agento11y\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" to pull the test suite, then invoke the agent using the starting prompt, record the scores, and assemble a report on the agent's performance. Test cases are identified by a unique ID that can be used to compare performance across two or more runs that include the same test cases. If you take an example with o11y-bench, using Haiku as a baseline, it’s clear that Sonnet is a vast improvement in quality for many of the test cases, but it increases the cost:\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]}]},\"component\":\"legacy_richtext\"},{\"_uid\":\"cee6fd3b-0aa7-400d-96da-b2811849fc9e\",\"media\":{\"id\":203431002380995,\"alt\":\"Experiments comparison view over two o11y-bench runs\",\"name\":\"\",\"focus\":\"\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"filename\":\"https://a-us.storyblok.com/f/1022730/1999x1050/a6a1a56f7d/agent-observability-best-practices-experiment-compare.png\",\"copyright\":\"\",\"fieldtype\":\"asset\",\"meta_data\":{\"alt\":\"Experiments comparison view over two o11y-bench runs\",\"size\":\"1999x1050\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"copyright\":\"\"},\"is_external_url\":false},\"caption\":\"Experiments comparison view over two o11y-bench runs\",\"alignment\":\"\",\"component\":\"legacy_image\"},{\"_uid\":\"d20efb0b-09d7-489d-8c2c-63869296de0f\",\"body\":{\"type\":\"doc\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\" (Try it out by publishing your own \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"o11y-bench run to Grafana\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://github.com/grafana/o11y-bench#publishing-results-to-agent-observability\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\").\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"You can trigger experiments manually or use them as CI/CD gates for pull requests to an agent repo for additional peace of mind. A baseline requirement for a pull request could be running an experiment in Grafana to prove there are no regressions, then pasting the link in the PR description.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]}]},\"component\":\"legacy_richtext\"},{\"_uid\":\"d81c6929-a5da-454c-8306-0d73c9f96c00\",\"media\":{\"id\":203431002364608,\"alt\":\"A failing CI/CD Github Action built on failing CI/CD Github Action built on Experiments\",\"size\":\"1998x1618\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"copyright\":\"\"},\"is_external_url\":false},\"caption\":\"A failing CI/CD GitHub Action Experiments\",\"alignment\":\"\",\"component\":\"legacy_image\"},{\"_uid\":\"0035a7c3-7340-4adb-84d1-cdcb16c25f3f\",\"body\":{\"type\":\"doc\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Once satisfied with results, the change can be merged and deployed, and the agent version can be monitored in the agents tab with the diff, in case a rollback is and Iterate\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"The final step is to repeat! Repeat the process of gathering data, improving the test suite, and testing out changes to the agent grounded in real data. As mentioned above, the field is constantly evolving. New models are released, new harness techniques are developed, and there is an infinite supply of prompt and tool configurations that could improve your agent. \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":2,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Try Agent Observability today\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Traditional observability has always been about maintaining and improving the way software runs. Agent observability takes it a step further by enabling developers to iterate on complex, non-deterministic agents by grounding results in real data.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"And with Grafana Agent Observability, which is now generally available in Grafana Cloud, you'll get peace of mind knowing you've got the tools you need to monitor and improve your agentic workloads. Grafana 13.2\",\"component\":\"legacy_cta\",\"is_external\":false},{\"_uid\":\"e70f7cd9-cd96-4c64-8324-5ba169e464cb\",\"body\":{\"type\":\"doc\",\"attrs\":{\"backgroundColor\":null},\"content\":[{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"In this post, we’ll highlight the latest updates to saved queries, a feature that lets teams share, discover, and reuse queries to get to trusted answers faster and help new teammates get up to speed. We’ll also explore how the new View panel sidebar makes exploring busy panels a breeze. \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"If you want to read about \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"all\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}},{\"type\":\"italic\"}]},{\"text\":\" the latest updates in Grafana 13.2, please refer to the \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"changelog\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://github.com/grafana/grafana/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1B55F5\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\" or our \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"What’s New documentation\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/docs/grafana/latest/whatsnew/whats-new-in-v13-2/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\". \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":2,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Saved queries: reuse trusted queries across dashboards and teams \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Good queries are hard-won. Writing one means knowing both the query language and your own data, like which of four similarly named metrics is the one you can trust. That knowledge usually sits with a few experienced people, or is gradually learned through exploration (increasingly AI-assisted), validation, and revision. Often teams end up rebuilding the same Grafana queries over and over, and the best ones live in pinned Slack messages or get copy-pasted from old dashboards. New team members feel it most, since their first weeks are often spent reverse-engineering existing dashboards just to work out how to ask a question of their own.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"The query history in Grafana Explore helps, keeping a couple of weeks of your own queries and letting you “star” the keepers. It's private to you, though. Until recently, there hasn't been a built-in way to take a query you trust and put it somewhere your whole organization can find it.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":3,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"How teams use saved queries \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#434343\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"We \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"built saved queries\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/blog/faster-more-collaborative-data-exploration-introducing-saved-queries-in-grafana-cloud/\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\", which is now generally available in Grafana Cloud and Grafana Enterprise, to address this challenge by providing a shared query library for your organization. When you write a query worth keeping, you can save it with a title, description, and tags. Saving works from dashboard panels, Explore, and annotation queries. This means teammates who don't know PromQL or SQL can still build dashboards from queries that the experts already vetted.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]}]},\"component\":\"legacy_richtext\"},{\"_uid\":\"3fd4926b-941c-4549-b3d0-d9ab70d93d46\",\"media\":{\"id\":210434146589448,\"alt\":\"A screenshot of an example saved queries library, with queries for billing and usage, last synced file, and more.\",\"name\":\"\",\"focus\":\"\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"filename\":\"https://a-us.storyblok.com/f/1022730/1999x1114/932a1346f1/grafana-13-2-saved-queries.png\",\"copyright\":\"\",\"fieldtype\":\"asset\",\"meta_data\":{\"alt\":\"A screenshot of an example saved queries library, with queries for billing and usage, last synced file, and more.\",\"title\":\"\",\"source\":\"\",\"copyright\":\"\"},\"is_external_url\":false},\"caption\":\"\",\"alignment\":\"\",\"component\":\"legacy_image\"},{\"_uid\":\"46855642-14f3-48e2-a471-f4bb6079ec99\",\"body\":{\"type\":\"doc\",\"attrs\":{\"backgroundColor\":null},\"content\":[{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Anyone in your organization can then browse the library in the saved queries dialog, searching by title, description, data source, or the query text itself, with filters for author and tags and the option to star favorites. From there, you can add a saved query to the panel you're editing, replace the query you're working on, or open it in Explore. And if a saved query contains variables, you can map them to your own dashboard's variables (or to plain values) when you reuse it, so a query written for one dashboard still works in another.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"Saved queries also \",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]},{\"text\":\"supports role-based access control\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://grafana.com/docs/grafana-cloud/learn-and-build/visualizations/panels-visualizations/query-transform-data/#roles-permissions-and-rbac\",\"uuid\":null,\"anchor\":null,\"target\":null,\"linktype\":\"url\"}},{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#1155CC\"}},{\"type\":\"underline\"}]},{\"text\":\", so you can choose who curates the library and who reuses from it.\",\"type\":\"text\",\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"textStyle\",\"attrs\":{\"color\":\"#000000\"}}]}]},{\"type\":\"heading\",\"attrs\":{\"level\":3,\"textAlign\":null},\"content\":[{\"text\":\"New ways to access saved queries and manage them as deployment.\"},\"slug\":\"how-to-scale-alloy-as-a-central-telemetry-gateway-capacity-planning-load-testing-and-production-lessons\",\"full_slug\":\"blog/how-to-scale-alloy-as-a-central-telemetry-gateway-capacity-planning-load-testing-and-production-lessons\",\"sort_by_date\":null,\"position\":-30790,\"tag_list\":[],\"is_startpage\":false,\"parent_id\":132709586468675,\"meta_data\":null,\"group_id\":\"cd5f0526-fa9d-42f2-97e7-2237cb9711a5\",\"first_published_at\":\"2026-08-21T15:12:52.230Z\",\"release_id\":null,\"lang\":\"default\",\"path\":null,\"alternates\":[],\"default_full_slug\":null,\"translated_slugs\":null}],\"tracking\":{\"pg\":\"blog\",\"plcmt\":\"featured-grafana-cloud\"}}]\n"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"5a:T1b94,"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"Thank you for spending AI Week with us. We’re thrilled by the reaction and we all enjoyed replying to your questions. Thanks for engaging. Some of my favorite quotes from LinkedIn and Reddit include: “Grafana’s latest announcements aren’t just 'AI features.' They signal a shift in what observability platforms are becoming.” “I had Claude migrate and update and validate dozens of hand-rolled dashboards… Worked astonishingly well… 95% done in 30m with the MCP and Claude.” Good vibes W hile AI Week is coming to a close, the story doesn't end here. Just like you, we're in the middle of a major transition in how AI and observability work together to solve problems quickly. We can’t do it without you. So please try our tools in your own teams, and let us know what we got right, as well as where we need to keep iterating. We know lots of people already feel left behind by the rate of change, so we’re making it our mission to catch you up, filter out all the noise, and bring you the very best in agentic operations. That's why I'd like to use this last post for the week to look back at how we got here and look ahead at what's yet to come—for Grafana Labs' AI tooling, for the software development and management industry, and most importantly, for users like you. Our story At Grafana Labs, we've always tried to innovate at the cutting edge of observability to solve real problems brought to us by our internal and external users. But when it comes to LLMs, we were a little late to the party. To be completely honest, we were skeptical . Starting in 2024, we began seeing so many examples of AI features being crammed in where they didn’t belong. And even when the use case made sense, the tools seemed incapable of executing the actual task. We realized things had changed when we started seeing LLMs running by themselves in loops; when agents were born. There is little doubt that agents have changed the way we build software. You don’t have to look far to find examples of people who have been supercharged by Claude, Codex, or Copilot, even if they aren’t loop-maxing , mob-vibing , or pitting agent against agent in adversarial arenas . We are building more and shipping more thanks to agents. Not only that, we’re also working in entirely new ways. What do you do while your agents are thinking ? Some engineers are working on multiple projects at the same time. And I mean that literally; while one agent cooks, they are prompting another. The rate of change has dramatically increased, and change, as we all know, is where trouble starts. Each engineer is now producing more operational demand than ever before and this shows no signs of slowing down. Naturally, at Grafana Labs, we feel like it falls to us to help you and your teams adapt and thrive in this new world. The AI team at Grafana Labs has grown significantly over the past year, but we remain dedicated to the experimentation and innovation that is already helping thousands of users today. We will continue to hold ourselves to the highest standard: we will never ship AI slop tools, or AI for the sake of it. To do this properly, we need your help. Your feedback, good and bad, helps ensure we’re solving real problems and it helps us continue to test our assumptions. A recap of AI Week Here’s a refresher of how the week went. Now generally available: Workspace : Your agent-native, full-page home for Assistant gcx and the Cloud MCP server : Structured Grafana access for you and your AI agents Assistant Investigations : Point it at an alert or incident and it investigates for you Automations : Automate your work on schedule Agent Observability : No more black-box agents. Watch what they do, evaluate their output, and spot problems early AI SDK (open source): Build your own AI agents and applications on top of Grafana Also in preview and ready to play with: Assistant Watchers : Agentic alerting for your systems Assistant Search : Search anything in plain language Assistant on mobile : Take Assistant with you, wherever you are Assistant on desktop (Private Preview): Assistant with access to your local files, code, and tools Assistant in Microsoft Teams (Private Preview): Use Assistant, right where your team works Assistant powered by OpenAI (Private Preview): Run Assistant on OpenAI models Agentic Testing (Experimental): Let agents test your websites for you Alongside the big reveals, the team also wrote about the thinking behind it all. There's the case for baking observability in from the very start , and how giving your coding agents access to your telemetry keeps you confident in what you ship . There's also how to automate the operational grind , and guidance on how to build trust in your agents , born from the challenge of scaling Grafana Assistant itself. You can find all of them over on the Grafana blog . We also lifted the lid on how some of this actually gets built. There are behind-the-scenes stories on gcx (our command line tool for humans and agents) and Assistant Search , and a look at how we build at Grafana Labs .Those can all be found on our engineering blog, Unprompted . So that’s a wrap for our first AI Week. Let us know how you think it went, what you’d like more of, and where we can improve if we do this again. On a personal note, I hope you see the dedication and creative spirit that the team is pouring into our new tools. We’re having a lot of fun building in this exciting space, and we can’t wait to hear from you after you have had time to kick the tires. What’s next? So, where do we go from here? Things are continuing to change out there at an exciting and daunting pace. We’re starting to trust AI on more meaningful tasks, and we’re seeing places it can run reliably as part of our workflows. We’re already impressed by some of the skills and MCP combinations people have set up for Assistant and Assistant Investigations, integrating it even deeper into their systems and teams. At Grafana Labs, we’re shaping a future where AI removes the daily operational grind, empowering engineers to focus on what’s next. As telemetry insights become universally accessible across the organization, we anticipate a shift toward more data-driven requests and deeper, enlightened discussions, ultimately allowing us to make better decisions. Auto-improvement loops can help optimize code based on your telemetry (which could be anything from infra metrics to business data), run A/B tests, and roll out the winning combination. Bug reports from users can be pre-investigated before a human has even seen it, enriching the support experience and speeding up resolution. Automated agents can carry out janitorial duties and generate intelligent reports that land in the right place at the right time. It's a lot to adapt to, but we plan to be there with you every step of the way. Thank you again for hanging out with us during AI Week, and please join us at ObservabilityCON 2026 in San Francisco this October for an update on all-things AI from Grafana Labs. "])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"28:[[\"$\",\"div\",null,{\"className\":\"rounded-lg bg-white p-4 md:p-12\",\"children\":[\"$\",\"$L44\",null,{\"title\":\"Reflections on AI Week, and the future of solving problems with observability and AI\",\"image\":\"https://a-us.storyblok.com/f/1022730/1200x628/b4e798caae/ai-week-lookback-meta.png\",\"authors\":[{\"name\":\"Unknown Author\",\"href\":\"#\"}],\"tags\":\"$2e:props:story:content:sections:0:posts:0:content:tags\",\"date\":\"2026-07-30 00:00\",\"href\":\"/blog/ai-week-recap?pg=blog\u0026plcmt=featured\",\"body\":\"$5a\",\"size\":\"md\",\"layout\":\"horizontal\",\"tracking\":{\"pg\":\"blog\",\"plcmt\":\"featured\"},\"description\":\"That's a wrap on our first AI Week, which was filled with announcements, conversations, and guidance for the new world of observability and AI. Let's look back—and
